---
title: "FreshBooks API reference"
sidebarTitle: "API"
description: "API reference for the FreshBooks plugin in Corsair: every `freshbooks.api.*` operation with its input and output types, taken from the plugin's Zod schemas."
---

<Info>
**New to Corsair?** See [API access](/concepts/api), [authentication](/concepts/auth), and [error handling](/concepts/error-handling).
</Info>

## Abilities

### list

`abilities.list`

Read what the signed-in identity may do in the business's projects.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.abilities.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `businessId` | `string \| number` | No | The numeric business id. Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the businessId option). |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `abilities` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="abilities full type">

```ts
{
  name?: any | null,
  value?: any | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

## Account Gateways

### delete

`accountGateways.delete`

Disconnect a payment gateway from the account.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.accountGateways.delete({ gatewayId: '12' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `gatewayId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The gateway id. |



**Output:** `object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts
{
} | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>


---

### list

`accountGateways.list`

List the payment gateways connected to the accounting account.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.accountGateways.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Results per page (default 15; FreshBooks caps every page at 100). |



**Output:** _empty object_


---

## Adjustment Journal Entries

### create

`adjustmentJournalEntries.create`

Create an adjustment journal entry: a description, a date and balanced debit and credit lines against ledger accounts.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.adjustmentJournalEntries.create({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `businessUuid` | `string \| number` | No | The business uuid. Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the businessUuid option). |



**Output:** _empty object_


---

### list

`adjustmentJournalEntries.list`

List adjustment journal entries of the business (ledger API, version 2023-09-25).

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.adjustmentJournalEntries.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `businessUuid` | `string \| number` | No | The business uuid. Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the businessUuid option). |
| `page_number` | `number` | No | Page number. |
| `page_size` | `number` | No | Results per page. |
| `start_date` | `string` | No | Entries on or after, YYYY-MM-DD. |
| `end_date` | `string` | No | Entries on or before, YYYY-MM-DD. |



**Output:** _empty object_


---

### update

`adjustmentJournalEntries.update`

Change an adjustment journal entry. (PUT /accounting/businesses/&#123;businessUuid&#125;/journal_entries/&#123;journalEntryUuid&#125;)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.adjustmentJournalEntries.update({ journalEntryUuid: '0b6f9d3e-1c2a-4e5b-9d8c-7a6b5c4d3e2f' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `businessUuid` | `string \| number` | No | The business uuid. Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the businessUuid option). |
| `journalEntryUuid` | `string \| number` | Yes | The journal entry uuid. |



**Output:** _empty object_


---

## Applications

### create

`applications.create`

Create an OAuth app (name, redirect URIs, scopes).

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.applications.create({
	application: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `application` | `object` | Yes | The application fields (FreshBooks wraps every body in its resource name). |
| `name` | `any` | No | — |
| `redirect_uri` | `any` | No | — |
| `description` | `any` | No | — |
| `website_url` | `any` | No | — |
| `settings_url` | `any` | No | — |
| `logo_public_id` | `any` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="application full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** _empty object_


---

### list

`applications.list`

List the OAuth apps the signed-in developer owns.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.applications.list({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `profile` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `first_name` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `last_name` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `email` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `language` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `confirmed_at` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `unconfirmed_email` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `setup_complete` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `phone_numbers` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `addresses` | `any[] \| null` | No | — |
| `profession` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `links` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `permissions` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `groups` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `subscription_statuses` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `integrations` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `business_memberships` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `identity_origin` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `roles` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="profile full type">

```ts
{
  setup_complete?: any | null,

} | null
```

## Callbacks

### create

`callbacks.create`

Register a webhook: `callback.event` (invoice.create, or a noun like invoice for all its events) and `callback.uri`. FreshBooks then POSTs a verification code to the URI; send it back with callbacks.verify.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.callbacks.create({
	callback: { event: 'invoice.create', uri: 'https://example.com/hooks/freshbooks' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `callback` | `object` | Yes | The callback fields (FreshBooks wraps every body in its resource name). |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="callback full type">

```ts
{
  event?: any,
  uri?: any
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** _empty object_


---

| `callbackId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The callback id. |



**Output:** `object`

```ts
} | {
```

### resendVerification

`callbacks.resendVerification`

Ask FreshBooks to post the verification code to the webhook URI again (also re-enables a webhook it disabled).

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.callbacks.resendVerification({ callbackId: '2001' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `callbackId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The callback id. |



**Output:** _empty object_


---

### verify

`callbacks.verify`

Activate a webhook with the verifier FreshBooks posted to its URI (`callback.verifier`). The verifier is also the secret that signs every delivery.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.callbacks.verify({
	callbackId: '2001',
	callback: { verifier: 'scADVVi5QuKuj5qTjVkbJNYQe7V7USpGd' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `callbackId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The callback id. |
| `callback` | `object` | Yes | The callback fields (FreshBooks wraps every body in its resource name). |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="callback full type">

```ts
{
  callback_id?: any,
  verifier?: any
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** _empty object_

## Clients

### create

`clients.create`

Create a client. Fields go in `client`. (POST /accounting/account/&#123;accountId&#125;/users/clients)

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.clients.create({
	client: {
		fname: 'Ada',
		lname: 'Lovelace',
		email: 'ada@example.com',
		organization: 'Analytical Engines',
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `include` | `(outstanding_balance \| credit_balance \| draft_balance \| overdue_balance \| grand_total_balance \| last_activity \| late_reminders \| late_fee)[]` | No | Extra data to include: outstanding_balance (include client's outstanding balance); credit_balance (include client's current balance of credit); draft_balance (include balance of draft invoices for client); overdue_balance (include overdue balance for the Client); grand_total_balance (include grand total for client); last_activity (include last activity); late_reminders (include information about late reminders); late_fee (include information about late fees) |
| `client` | `object` | Yes | The client fields (FreshBooks wraps every body in its resource name). |

### list

`clients.list`

List clients, newest first, with search filters, includes and paging.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.clients.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Results per page (default 15; FreshBooks caps every page at 100). |
| `search` | `object` | No | Search filters, sent as search[&lt;name&gt;]=&lt;value&gt;: address_like, email, has_credit, has_draft, has_outstanding, has_overdue, fname_like, lname_like, note_like, organization_like, phone_like, updated_min, updated_max, user_like, userid, userids, username, vis_state, email_like |
| `include` | `(outstanding_balance \| credit_balance \| draft_balance \| overdue_balance \| grand_total_balance \| last_activity \| late_reminders \| late_fee)[]` | No | Extra data to include: outstanding_balance (include client's outstanding balance); credit_balance (include client's current balance of credit); draft_balance (include balance of draft invoices for client); overdue_balance (include overdue balance for the Client); grand_total_balance (include grand total for client); last_activity (include last activity); late_reminders (include information about late reminders); late_fee (include information about late fees) |
| `sort` | `string` | No | Sort field, `<field>_asc` or `<field>_desc` (fields: client_id, organization_name, email, bus_phone, updated, fullname, primarycontact, outstanding, overdue, credit). |

Delete a credit note (goodwill, prepayment or refund credit) (sets vis_state to 1). FreshBooks keeps it: an update with vis_state 0 restores it.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.creditNotes.delete({ creditId: '30947' });
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `creditId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The credit id. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `credit_note` | `object \| null` | No | — |

### update

`creditNotes.update`

Change fields of a credit note (goodwill, prepayment or refund credit). FreshBooks updates one phase of fields per call: send vis_state on its own.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.creditNotes.update({
	creditId: '30947',
	credit_note: {},
});
```

### sendEmail

`estimates.sendEmail`

Email the estimate now, to `estimate.email_recipients` with an optional customized subject and body. Cannot be unsent.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.estimates.sendEmail({
	estimateId: '11223',
	estimate: {},
});
```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `estimateId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The estimate id. |
| `estimate` | `object` | Yes | The estimate fields (FreshBooks wraps every body in its resource name). action_email true is added for you. |

### update

`estimates.update`

Change fields of a estimate. FreshBooks updates one phase of fields per call: send vis_state on its own.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
	estimateId: '11223',
	estimate: {},
});
```

| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `estimateId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The estimate id. |

### update

`expenses.update`

Change fields of a expense. FreshBooks updates one phase of fields per call: send vis_state on its own.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.expenses.update({
	expenseId: '99881',
	expense: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `expenseId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The expense id. |
| `include` | `(attachment \| category \| expense_profile \| project \| account)[]` | No | Extra data to include: attachment (Includes attachment details assocatied with expense receipts); category (Include expense category in expense); expense_profile (Include expense profile info in expense); project (Include related projects in expense); account (Include account info in expense) |

## Invoice Profiles

### create

`invoiceProfiles.create`

| `per_page` | `number` | No | Results per page (default 15; FreshBooks caps every page at 100). |
| `search` | `object` | No | Search filters, sent as search[&lt;name&gt;]=&lt;value&gt;. |
| `include` | `(allowed_gateways \| audit_logs \| contacts \| late_fee \| lines \| presentation \| system \| tracking \| next_issue_date \| project_format)[]` | No | Extra data to include: allowed_gateways (list of allowed gateways); audit_logs (a summarization of the invoice for audit purposes); contacts (contains contact objects populated with the client's data); late_fee (late fee info); lines (invoice lines); presentation (data representing the stylings of the invoice. i.e. (Modern vs Classic)); system (invoice-owner's system); tracking (information regarding previous invoices); next_issue_date (the date that the next invoice will be issued); project_format (includes information about how the associated project is formated) |
| `sort` | `string` | No | Sort field, `<field>_asc` or `<field>_desc`. |

```ts
}
```

### update

`invoiceProfiles.update`

Change fields of a recurring invoice profile. FreshBooks updates one phase of fields per call: send vis_state on its own.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.invoiceProfiles.update({
	invoiceProfileId: '4321',
	invoice_profile: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `invoiceProfileId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The invoice profile id. |
| `include` | `(allowed_gateways \| audit_logs \| contacts \| late_fee \| lines \| presentation \| system \| tracking \| next_issue_date \| project_format)[]` | No | Extra data to include: allowed_gateways (list of allowed gateways); audit_logs (a summarization of the invoice for audit purposes); contacts (contains contact objects populated with the client's data); late_fee (late fee info); lines (invoice lines); presentation (data representing the stylings of the invoice. i.e. (Modern vs Classic)); system (invoice-owner's system); tracking (information regarding previous invoices); next_issue_date (the date that the next invoice will be issued); project_format (includes information about how the associated project is formated) |
| `invoice_profile` | `object` | Yes | The invoice profile fields (FreshBooks wraps every body in its resource name). |

## Invoices

### create

`invoices.create`

Create a invoice. Fields go in `invoice`.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.invoices.create({
	invoice: {
		customerid: 31006,
		create_date: '2026-11-28',
		lines: [{
			type: 0,
			name: 'Consulting',
			qty: 2,
			unit_cost: { amount: '150.00', code: 'USD' },
		}],
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `include` | `(audit_logs \| client_audits \| comments \| contacts \| lines \| owner \| latest_journal_entry \| allowed_gateways \| late_fee \| presentation \| project_total_amounts \| late_reminders \| system)[]` | No | Extra data to include: audit_logs (Show invoice view/send/payment history); client_audits (Show the client-visible invoice history); comments (Comments made on invoice); contacts (List of client contacts); lines (Invoice lines); owner (Owner info); latest_journal_entry (Journal entry info); allowed_gateways (List of allowed gateways); late_fee (Late fee info); presentation (Presentation type); project_total_amounts (Total amounts billed from projects); late_reminders (Late reminders); system (Invoice-owner's system) |
| `invoice` | `object` | Yes | The invoice fields (FreshBooks wraps every body in its resource name). |

<Accordion title="invoice full type">

```ts
  create_date?: any,

    type?: any,
    description?: any,

    taxAmount1?: any,
    name?: any,
    qty?: any,
    taxName2?: any,
    taxAmount2?: any,
    unit_cost?: {
      amount?: any,
      code?: any
    },
    expenseid?: any
  }[],
  presentation?: {
    image_logo_src?: any,
    theme_primary_color?: any,
    theme_layout?: any,
    theme_font_name?: any
  }
}
```

### delete

`invoices.delete`

Delete a invoice (sets vis_state to 1). FreshBooks keeps it: an update with vis_state 0 restores it.

**Risk:** `destructive`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.invoices.delete({ invoiceId: '987654' });
```

### downloadPdf

`invoices.downloadPdf`

Download the invoice as a PDF. (GET /accounting/account/&#123;accountId&#125;/invoices/invoices/&#123;invoiceId&#125;/pdf)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.invoices.downloadPdf({ invoiceId: '987654' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `invoiceId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The invoice id. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### sendEmail

`invoices.sendEmail`

Email the invoice now, to `invoice.email_recipients` (or the client) with an optional `invoice_customized_email` subject and body. Cannot be unsent.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.invoices.sendEmail({
	invoiceId: '987654',
	invoice: {
		email_recipients: ['billing@example.com'],
	},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `invoiceId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The invoice id. |
| `invoice` | `object` | Yes | The invoice fields (FreshBooks wraps every body in its resource name). action_email true is added for you. |

```ts
{
  email_recipients?: any[],
  invoice_customized_email?: {

  },
  action_email?: any
}
```

### update

`invoices.update`

Change fields of a invoice. FreshBooks updates one phase of fields per call: send vis_state on its own.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.invoices.update({
	invoiceId: '987654',
	invoice: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `invoiceId` | `string \| number` | Yes | The invoice id. |
| `include` | `(audit_logs \| client_audits \| comments \| contacts \| lines \| owner \| latest_journal_entry \| allowed_gateways \| late_fee \| presentation \| project_total_amounts \| late_reminders \| system)[]` | No | Extra data to include: audit_logs (Show invoice view/send/payment history); client_audits (Show the client-visible invoice history); comments (Comments made on invoice); contacts (List of client contacts); lines (Invoice lines); owner (Owner info); latest_journal_entry (Journal entry info); allowed_gateways (List of allowed gateways); late_fee (Late fee info); presentation (Presentation type); project_total_amounts (Total amounts billed from projects); late_reminders (Late reminders); system (Invoice-owner's system) |
| `invoice` | `object` | Yes | The invoice fields (FreshBooks wraps every body in its resource name). |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="invoice full type">

```ts
  status?: any,
  lines?: {
    type?: any,
    description?: any,
    expenseid?: any,
    taxName1?: any,

    name?: any,
    qty?: any,
    taxName2?: any,
    taxAmount2?: any,
    unit_cost?: {
      amount?: any,
      code?: any
    }
  }[],
  allowed_gatewayids?: any[]
}
```

Read the account's default online payment options.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.paymentOptions.getDefault({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |

**Output:** _empty object_

| `page` | `number` | No | Page number. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Results per page (default 15; FreshBooks caps every page at 100). |
| `active` | `boolean` | No | True marks that the project is still an active project. |
| `complete` | `boolean` | No | True marks that the project has been completed. |
| `include_logged_duration` | `boolean` | No | True returns the time logged for a project (in seconds). |
| `updated_since` | `string` | No | Matches projects that have been updated at or after the specified utc date/time (ISO 8601, UTC). |
| `skip_group` | `boolean` | No | True returns projects without the group field. |
| `sort` | `string` | No | Sort field (created_at, due_date, title); prefix with - for descending. |

### download

`reports.download`

Download a report as CSV with the download token a report call returned (fileName like invoice_details.csv).

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.reports.download({ downloadToken: 'd0wnl0adt0ken', fileName: 'invoice_details.csv' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `downloadToken` | `string \| number` | Yes | The download token. |
| `fileName` | `string \| number` | Yes | The file name. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

## Retainers

### create

`retainers.create`

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.retainers.create({
	retainer: {},
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `businessId` | `string \| number` | No | The numeric business id. Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the businessId option). |
| `retainer` | `object` | Yes | The retainer fields (FreshBooks wraps every body in its resource name). |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="retainer full type">

```ts
  client_user_id?: any,
  start_date?: any,
  next_period_start_date?: any,

  excess_rate?: any,
  budgeted_time?: any,
  active?: any,
  frequency?: any,
  number_recurring?: any,
  is_infinitely_recurring?: any
```

### list

`timeEntries.list`

List time entries with filters (billed, client, teammate, date range) and paging.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.timeEntries.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `businessId` | `string \| number` | No | The numeric business id. Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the businessId option). |
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Results per page (default 15; FreshBooks caps every page at 100). |
| `billable` | `boolean` | No | True returns entries that can be automatically added to an invoice. |
| `billed` | `boolean` | No | True returns entries that have already been added to an invoice. |
| `client_id` | `string` | No | Matches exact client id. |
| `include_deleted` | `boolean` | No | True returns deleted time entries. |
| `team` | `boolean` | No | True returns entries logged by all members of the business. |
| `include_unlogged` | `boolean` | No | True returns entries currently in progress for a running timer. |
| `started_from` | `string` | No | Matches entries that take place at or after the specified UTC date/time (ISO 8601, UTC). |
| `started_to` | `string` | No | Matches entries that take place before or at the specified UTC date/time (ISO 8601, UTC). |
| `updated_since` | `string` | No | Matches entries that have been updated at or after the specified UTC date/time (ISO 8601, UTC). |
| `identity_id` | `string` | No | Matches entries logged against a specific teammate or user. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `meta` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `time_entries` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="meta full type">

```ts
{
  total_logged?: any | null,
  total_unbilled?: any | null,
  total?: any | null,
  per_page?: any | null,
  page?: any | null,
  pages?: any | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="time_entries full type">

```ts
{
  id?: any | null,
  identity_id?: any | null,
  timer?: any | null,
  is_logged?: any | null,
  started_at?: any | null,
  created_at?: any | null,
  duration?: any | null,
  client_id?: any | null,
  project_id?: any | null,
  pending_client?: any | null,
```

### revoke

`tokens.revoke`

Revoke an OAuth refresh or access token. The connection stops working until it is authorized again.

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.tokens.revoke({ client_id: 'app-client-id', client_secret: 'app-client-secret', token: 'refresh-token-to-revoke' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `client_id` | `string` | Yes | The OAuth app's client id. |
| `client_secret` | `string` | Yes | The OAuth app's client secret. |
| `token` | `string` | Yes | The refresh token (or access token) to revoke. |



**Output:** _empty object_


---

## Uploads

### attachment

`uploads.attachment`

Upload an attachment (an expense receipt, an invoice attachment). Returns the jwt to reference it on the expense or invoice.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.uploads.attachment({
	content: { base64: 'SGVsbG8gZnJvbSBDb3JzYWly', filename: 'notes.txt', contentType: 'text/plain' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `content` | `string \| object \| custom` | Yes | The file to upload (form field `content`). |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="content full type">

```ts
string | {
  url: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | {
  base64: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | custom
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** _empty object_


---

### image

`uploads.image`

Upload an image (an invoice logo or banner, a proposal image). Returns the jwt to reference it and a link.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.uploads.image({
	content: { base64: 'SGVsbG8gZnJvbSBDb3JzYWly', filename: 'notes.txt', contentType: 'text/plain' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `accountId` | `string \| number` | No | The accounting account id (like LJArJ4). Left out, the plugin reads it from users.me (or the accountId option). |
| `content` | `string \| object \| custom` | Yes | The file to upload (form field `content`). |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="content full type">

```ts
string | {
  url: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | {
  base64: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | custom
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `image` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `link` | `any \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="image full type">

```ts
{
  jwt?: any | null,
  public_id?: any | null,
  filename?: any | null
} | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



---

### publicImage

`uploads.publicImage`

Upload an image that belongs to no account (an app listing logo). Returns its jwt and link.

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.uploads.publicImage({
	content: { base64: 'SGVsbG8gZnJvbSBDb3JzYWly', filename: 'notes.txt', contentType: 'text/plain' },
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `content` | `string \| object \| custom` | Yes | The file to upload (form field `content`). |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="content full type">

```ts
string | {
  url: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | {
  base64: string,
  filename?: string,
  contentType?: string
} | custom
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output:** _empty object_

## Users

### me

`users.me`

Read the signed-in identity: name, email, and business_memberships with each business id, business_uuid and accounting account_id the other operations need.

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.freshbooks.api.users.me({});
```

**Input:** _empty object_


**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `profile` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `first_name` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `last_name` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `email` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `language` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `confirmed_at` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `unconfirmed_email` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `setup_complete` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `phone_numbers` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `addresses` | `any[] \| null` | No | — |
| `profession` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `links` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `permissions` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `groups` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `subscription_statuses` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `integrations` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `business_memberships` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `identity_origin` | `any \| null` | No | — |
| `roles` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
